# Volume 06 Batch 0004 — Character state at Chapter 290

**Additive to every section above. Volume 06 is open. Batch 0005 inherits this.**

## A man of about thirty-seven who puts tables up for anybody who will use them

**NEW TO THE PROSE IN THREE HUNDRED CHAPTERS AND DESCRIBED AND NEVER NAMED.** He came down the eleven mile road on the thirtieth of the sixth month with a hand-cart and a tent rolled on it, gave no name to anybody in this district, and was not asked for one. **He asked the yard of Lot Seventeen for its address on the first of the seventh month and named the fifth of the five things this district does not have out loud in that yard in about four seconds. On the second he put five lines on the end of a trestle table and had them read out and read back, and when a man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said the fifth of them is not true he agreed with him out loud in the same yard and said he wrote it on purpose and would like it read again so that nobody could say it was hidden in a hole. On the third he named eleven coppers a week. On the sixth he asked a yard out loud whether anybody in this district carries water to a house in the ground and four people looked round at a woman of fifty-eight, and at half past four that day he said from the top of a bank that he is not going to ask about that house again because a house in the ground does not answer a door. On the ninth he refused a figure of two mornings on the ground that a figure of two mornings is a figure about two mornings. On the tenth he could not lift a trestle board off his own cart with either hand and said so out loud in a hundred and thirty-seven words before anybody worked it out of him.** He is the shape this volume's method is wearing this month and he is **not a new final enemy, not a keeper of anything, not a bearer of a document, not a party of anything, and not a party of the second of the two books in the room where the books are, which nobody has read.**

## The man the figure of twenty-one years is against

**HE OFFERED THE BEST USE ANYBODY HAS FOUND FOR THAT BOOK IN FIVE MONTHS AND WAS REFUSED IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE, AND HE DID NOT TAKE IT BACK.** 288. The offer was to put both sheets into the first of the two books in the room four hundred yards from that gate, with the day on them and the reason printed on the face of it in her hand, because a wrong thing that is on the record of a district is a thing a body four hundred miles off has to read before it does anything about it. He said the room out loud before he said anything else, because he had learned that in this district last month. **He said in a yard at 285, in two hundred and eighty-nine words, that a market that is the address of both sides cannot be the address of either side, and that he has got no third thing and is not going to stand in a yard and pretend he has found one. He said in a lane at 289, uncounted, that he got nothing from a woman of about thirty-one and that he is not going to ask her a fourth time and is not going to find another way round her, and that he would like that entered as a thing he has said out loud to a man who will not remember it by the end of the week.** He is a Convener and the promotion is not a screen and the price is being paid. He is not a founder, not a holder, not a bearer of a toll, not a party of anything, and findable. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO CONVENES NINE SETTLEMENTS IS NOT A REPRESENTATIVE OF A SETTLEMENT THAT HAS NOT ASKED TO BE REPRESENTED, AND THAT A MAN WHO HAS BEEN REFUSED NINE THINGS IN FIVE MONTHS BY THREE WOMEN AND HAS ACQUIRED NOTHING BY ANY OF THE NINE IS NOT A PARTY OF ANY OF THEM.**

## The registrar of this district's records office

**SHE REFUSED A MAN'S HELP IN A ROOM WITH A SHUT DOOR IN IT, IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE, AND THE REASON SHE GAVE WAS ABOUT THE BOOK AND NOT ABOUT HIM.** 288. I have kept that book in this room nine years and I know exactly what is in it, every line, in the order the lines are in, and the reason that room is safe is not the shut door. The shut door is nothing. **If the fifth of those five lines goes on a page in that book with a day on it, then this basin has written down, in a place a stranger can walk up to, that eleven coppers a week was offered to people who are not in households on a morning in the seventh month, and the fifth line says a person who stood at that table once cannot be uncounted again, and the third of those five lines, which is the only line on that sheet worth having, would be undone by the second sheet we put in beside it. You would be publishing the morning.** She told him not to come and ask her again this month. She is not a bearer, not a holder, not a steward and not a party of anything, and a keeper of a room is not a thing she is and has never been asked to be. **HER EARLIER POSITION IS UNCHANGED AND WAS STATED AGAIN IN THAT ROOM: there is no room in this district that nobody can walk into.** The second book on that shelf has been open since the sixth of last month and was not read in this block and nothing was entered in it.

## The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing

**HE READ THE OFFER OUT IN A YARD AND SAID THE FIFTH OF ITS FIVE LINES IS NOT TRUE AND CANNOT BE MADE TRUE, AND HE AGREED WITH THE MAN WHO WROTE IT AND DID NOT ARGUE WITH HIM, AND HE READ THE FOUR LINES OF PRINT OUT AS A THING HE HAD READ AND NOT AS A THING READ OUT IN THAT YARD.** 282, 284. **HE CARRIED THE OTHER END OF A HAND-CART THROUGH A GATE THAT TAKES TWO GOES ON THE FIRST OF THIS MONTH AND COULD NOT TAKE THE WEIGHT ON HIS LEFT HAND AND WENT UP THE BANK WITH IT ON HIS RIGHT SHOULDER INSTEAD, AND SAID SO OUT LOUD IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS, AND THE SHOULDER HAS NOT BEEN WHAT IT WAS SINCE THE FIRST OF THIS MONTH. ON THE FOURTH HE STOOD AT THE TOP OF A THREE FOOT DITCH AND PUT A HAND DOWN AND HAD A MAN OUT OF IT BY THE ARM, AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES.** **ON THE SIXTH HE SAID NOBODY IS TO ANSWER THE QUESTION ABOUT A HOUSE IN THE GROUND BEFORE ANYBODY ELSE IN THAT YARD HAD SAID ANYTHING, AND ON THE EIGHTH HE WAS TOLD ONE SENTENCE AT A DOOR AND SAID HE WAS NOT GOING TO SAY WHETHER THE MAN WAS RIGHT.** He ended the block in a lane behind a bank at about half past seven on the last night in two hundred and eighty-four words, and the last of them is: **I do not know what that gate is yet. I know what it is not and I have said so out loud in a lane and I am not going to be the man who writes it down as something else.** He is not a founder and is not a keeper of anything and has never been asked to be one.

## The man of about thirty-four who digs loam

**HE REFUSED ELEVEN COPPERS A WEEK IN A YARD IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE ON THE THIRD OF THIS MONTH, AND THAT REFUSAL MOVED THE COUNT OF REFUSALS OF ELEVEN COPPERS A WEEK FROM EIGHT TO NINE, AND HE SAID THE REASON WAS HIS OWN HAND AND THE SHAPE OF WHAT HE HAS ALREADY BEEN IN: I have been in about four of them in five months and I am not going to be in another one, and I am not going to be one of a number of anything at a table either.** 283. **HE WALKED ELEVEN MILES OUT AND ELEVEN MILES BACK IN ABOUT ELEVEN HOURS ON THE SECOND AND FOUND THAT THERE IS NO LEAN-TO ON THE FAR SIDE OF THE ELEVEN MILE ROAD, ONLY A WALL ABOUT FOUR FEET HIGH WITH A GATE IN IT THAT IS NOT ON A HINGE, AND PUT HIS HAND DOWN IN A DITCH FOR ABOUT AN HOUR. ON THE FOURTH HE WENT DOWN INTO THE DITCH BEHIND THE BUILDING WITH TWO DOORS AND DUG OUT ABOUT FOUR FEET OF IT AND WAS IN THE BOTTOM FOR ABOUT THREE HOURS AND COULD NOT GET HIMSELF OUT AND WAS PULLED OUT BY THE ARM.** 284. **He said, uncounted at 282, that the second of those five lines is the only line on the sheet doing anything, and that he did that count to about nine people at two in the morning on the night of the twenty-fourth of last month and did not know one of their names, and that he has not got an argument against the second of them and has got one against the fifth.** On the tenth he lifted a trestle board off a hand-cart and put it against a wall and said nothing at all about it. He is not one of the five and does not live in any of their rooms.

## The man of fifty-six

**HE SAID IN A YARD THAT A MARKET IS A PLACE WHERE A PERSON IS ASKED A QUESTION AND THAT IN TEN DAYS THE ONLY QUESTIONS ANYBODY HAS ASKED IN THAT YARD HAVE BEEN ABOUT WHAT THAT YARD IS, AND THAT THE PROBLEM IS NOT THAT TWO PEOPLE ARE PULLING AT THE GATE BUT THAT THE GATE HAS NEVER HAD A QUESTION IN IT.** 285. **HE DISAGREED WITH THE ONE SYSTEM PANEL IN THIS BLOCK OUT LOUD IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS AND SAID IT WAS THE FIFTH SENTENCE OF HIS OWN THAT HAD BEEN SAID BACK TO HIM IN TWENTY DAYS AND THAT HE HAD NOT SAID IT ONCE, AND SAID HE WAS NOT GOING TO TAKE IT OFF THE SHELF IT IS ON.** 286. **He said in a yard that four hundred and eleven is a count of the people who answered a door, and that a count of the people who come to a table is not a lie, and that in about five months somebody four hundred miles off will hold two counts of the same people and the two will not be the same figure and neither will be a lie.** 283. **At half past four on the sixth he said one thing back, uncounted, about a house in the ground and about a table being a door with a roof on it.** He is right and says so with no pleasure in it.

## The woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale

**SHE SAID THE WORD RATE OUT LOUD IN A YARD BEFORE ANYBODY ASKED HER FOR IT, SAID IT TWICE, AND THEN SAID THE REST OF IT IN TWO HUNDRED AND EIGHTEEN WORDS.** 283. Eleven coppers a week is not a price. A price is a thing you are told and a rate is a thing that is already true of you whether anybody says it or not, and if that man stands a table up eleven miles off and counts the people who come to it, then in four months there is no such thing as a person in this district who is not in a household. **I weigh about nine sacks a day and I have never weighed a day. I have four empty houses standing up that lane and I said on the twentieth of last month they are not going to be offered again. I am not standing at a table to be one of a figure and I am saying so before anybody asks me.** She has stood at that scale nine years and she is the person who asked the question that exposed a borrowed word and **she supplied neither word; the closed two-words canon is unchanged and is to be kept exactly.** Her four empty houses are not going to be offered again before the first day of the season after this one, on her own statement, which she repeated on the third of the seventh month.

## The man of about forty-eight who keeps a tally for six households

**A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT. NOT THIRTY-FOUR, and he says himself in Chapter 184 that he is fifty-one.** On the ninth of the seventh month he came down the lane behind the bank with his book under his arm on his own two feet and went past the yard of Lot Seventeen without stopping, and about four people said good morning to him and he said good morning back and said nothing else. **A CLERK ENTERED THE FIGURE OF HIS SIX OUT OF A LADDER, SEVENTY-NINE DAYS, AND SAID SHE DID NOT ASK HIM FOR IT THAT MORNING AND WILL NOT ASK HIM FOR IT THIS MONTH, AND HE SAID THE FIGURE ON NONE OF THE TEN DAYS OF THIS BLOCK.** A figure a man will not repeat is not a refusal and is not a consent. A man who keeps a count of six households is not a clerk of a house and is not a bearer of a book and is not a keeper of anything.

## The man of about thirty-four who keeps a road

**HE SAID IN A YARD ON THE FIRST OF THIS MONTH, IN A HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-SIX WORDS, THAT A YARD IS A GOOD ADDRESS FOR A PERSON WHO ALREADY LIVES IN THE DISTRICT AND A USELESS ONE FOR A PERSON WHO DOES NOT, BECAUSE A PERSON WHO IS NOT IN A HOUSEHOLD DOES NOT KNOW WHICH OF THE NINE SETTLEMENTS YOU ARE IN, AND THE MAN WHO ASKED THE YARD HAD BEEN DOWN FOUR DAYS OF ROAD AND HAD NOT SAID WHICH OF THEM HE CAME OUT OF.** 281. **HE REFUSED A JOIN ON THE FIFTH IN A HUNDRED AND THIRTY-SIX WORDS AND GAVE BOTH HALVES: two documents on one page do not make them agree, they make one thing a man can carry, and if those two are on one page then everybody who stands at that table is standing under both of them at once, and neither the man of about thirty-seven nor the body four hundred miles off has thought about what that does to the people he is trying to pay.** **ON THE SEVENTH HE SAID, IN A HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-FOUR WORDS, THAT A ROAD DOES NOT GO WHERE A SHEET SAYS IT GOES, AND THAT THERE IS A TURNING OFF THE ELEVEN MILE ROAD ABOUT A MILE BEFORE THAT WALL AND IT IS NOT ON THE SHEET.** He is not a bearer of the road and has never been asked to be one.

## The woman of about thirty-four who keeps a goat

**SHE IS A WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR AND THIRTY-FOUR IS CLOSED CANON.** She is at the third house up the lane from the scale. **ON THE SECOND OF THIS MONTH SHE CARRIED A COPY OF THE OFFER'S FIVE LINES UP TWO MILES TO A ROOM ABOUT NINE FEET BY FOURTEEN WITH NOTHING OVER THE DOOR SO THAT ABOUT NINE PEOPLE IN THERE COULD HEAR WHAT HAD BEEN SAID IN THE YARD, AND NOBODY ASKED HER, AND THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THAT ROOM DID NOT SEND FOR HER AND IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS SENT FOR.** 282. **ON THE SEVENTH SHE SAID ONE THING AT THE FOOT OF HER OWN GATE AND IT WAS NOT ABOUT AN ARM: My gate has not been off its post since I was a girl and I have never once seen it work and I have never once needed it to. That man has got a gate in a wall four feet high on the far side of eleven miles of road and it is not on a hinge, and a gate that is not on a hinge is a gate somebody can be counted coming through, and I am not going to say that twice.** She is not a clerk of a house and not a keeper of a gate and not a bearer of anything.

## The woman of about twenty-nine who keeps the room with nothing over the door

**NOT A SPEAKER IN THIS BLOCK AND NOT ENTERED IN IT, AND THE ONLY THING SHE DOES IS THAT A ROOM OF HERS IS WHERE A COPY OF THE FIVE LINES WAS READ OUT.** 282. **The room is about nine feet by fourteen, there is nothing over the door, and about nine people sleep there most nights. A clerk entered that nobody asked the woman of about thirty-four to carry it and that the woman who keeps that room did not send for her. THE CHILD OF ABOUT EIGHT IS NOT IN THIS BLOCK AND IS NOT USED IN IT.** She is not a clerk of a house and not a bearer of a bench and not a keeper of anything.

## A child of about eight

**NOT IN CHAPTERS 281 TO 290 AND NOT SPENT AGAIN. THE LITERAL STRING *A CHILD* IS AT ZERO ACROSS THE TEN CHAPTERS AGAINST SEVENTEEN IN CHAPTERS 271 TO 280.** He is not asked anything by anybody in this block and no chapter of this block names him or uses him as an example. **HE IS NOT THE BOY OF NINE WHOSE NAME IS IN A COLUMN IN A BUILDING TWO DAYS OF ROAD AWAY, AND THE TWO ARE NOT THE SAME PERSON AND ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER.**

## The man of about twenty-three who sells nothing and copies for nothing

**HIS HAND HAS NOT BEEN RIGHT SINCE THE THIRTEENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND ON THE FIFTH OF THE SEVENTH IT STOPPED HIM PRODUCING ANYTHING AT ALL: he got through two lines of the copy of the four lines of print and could not do the third, and he put the piece of paper down on the end of the trestle table with the chalk mark of the third line started, and he said in a hundred and thirty words that he offered before anybody asked him and that he is not going to be able to do the rest of it this month.** 285. **ON THE SEVENTH, AT HALF PAST FOUR, HE READ FOUR LINES OF THE PRINTED SHEET OUT ALOUD IN ABOUT NINE FEET OF LANE OUTSIDE THE OFFICE TO ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE, BECAUSE HE CANNOT COPY THEM AND A THING THAT IS ONLY EVER READ BY ONE MAN IN A YARD STOPS WHEN THAT MAN IS ILL, AND THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE BOOKS IN THAT ROOM WAS FOUR FEET AWAY WITH HER HAND ON THE DOOR AND DID NOT STOP HIM.** He is not a clerk of a house and is not a bearer of a copy and is not a keeper of anything. **A clerk entered that a hand that stops in the middle of a document is a hand and is not a refusal and is not a consent, and that a man reading four lines out loud is not a copy of it and is not a document.**

## A man of about nineteen who counts

**HE COUNTED FOUR PEOPLE IN A YARD TURNING ROUND TO LOOK AT A WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT AND GAVE THE NUMBER OUT LOUD, AND HE COUNTED THE HEADS AT A GATE AND GOT NINE AND GAVE THAT OUT LOUD WITHOUT BEING ASKED.** 286, 289. **A CLERK ENTERED BOTH TIMES THAT A NUMBER A MAN OF ABOUT NINETEEN COUNTS IN DAYLIGHT IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE IS A FIGURE ABOUT THE COUNTING AND NOT ABOUT THE PEOPLE, AND THAT FOUR PEOPLE LOOKING ROUND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT AND A HEAD COUNTED AT A GATE IN DAYLIGHT IS A FIGURE ABOUT A GATE.** He is nobody's clerk and is not a counter of people.

## The woman of fifty-eight

**SHE WAS AT THE BANK WITH TWO BUCKETS AT ABOUT TEN IN THE MORNING ON THE SIXTH OF THIS MONTH AND WAS ASKED NO QUESTION BY ANYBODY. FOUR PEOPLE IN A YARD LOOKED ROUND AT HER BECAUSE A STRANGER HAD ASKED A QUESTION SHE WAS THE ONLY PERSON THERE WHO COULD ANSWER, AND SHE ANSWERED IT HERSELF IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS, BEFORE SHE WAS ASKED, AND SAID THE REST OF IT IN TWO HUNDRED AND FIVE WORDS.** 286. **Yes. I have carried water to eleven houses for nineteen years and the ninth of those eleven is a house in the ground and it is in the shadow of a building I can see two ends of from a rise a mile out past the loams, and the woman in it is not one of the four hundred and eleven on that sheet at your gatepost and she is not on any sheet, and she asked me for a second bucket in the second week of last month and I put a second bucket there on the third of that month. So she is not on the list of the people who are to be moved and she has never been asked a question by anybody in nineteen years and I have asked her about a bucket twice and I am not going to ask her anything else today.** **SHE WAS NOT NAMED BY ANYBODY. THE COST WAS BEING LOOKED AT AND SHE PAID IT BY ANSWERING RATHER THAN BY BEING ASKED, AND SHE SAID OUT LOUD THAT BEING LOOKED AT IS WORSE THAN SAYING IT.** Her two buckets were not counted on any of the ten days. **THE NINTH OF THE ELEVEN IS ONE OF THE FIVE DIFFERENT NINTHS AND A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN HAS SAID OUT LOUD THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO ASK ABOUT IT AGAIN.**

## The man of about twenty-nine who drives a cart

**HE READ THE OFFER'S FIVE LINES BACK IN A YARD AND GOT NOTHING WRONG, WHICH IS THE RULE OF THE COUNTER, AND HE WAS ASKED TO READ IT BACK AND WAS NOT ASKED WHETHER HE AGREED WITH IT.** 282. **HE HAULED A HAND-CART, A TABLE AND A TENT ELEVEN MILES OUT TO THAT WALL ON THE FIFTH OF THIS MONTH AND ELEVEN MILES BACK ON THE SIXTH, FOR NOTHING, AND SAID IT IN A HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-SEVEN WORDS: two days of my cart, and a load of stone I was paid for and did not take, and the man who paid for it has it by another road and I do not know whether that is going to cost me the road after next. I have not been paid for the two days and he did not offer and I did not ask, because when I saw that wall I understood there is not going to be anything to be paid for, there is going to be a table on it.** 287. **HE IS NOT THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A ROOM WITH NOTHING OVER THE DOOR AND NOT THE MAN OF ABOUT NINETEEN WHO COUNTS, AND HE ASKED FOR IT TO BE ENTERED AND GOT IT. HE WAS NOT IN THIS DISTRICT ON THE FIFTH AND THE SIXTH OF THIS MONTH AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT THOSE ARE TWO DAYS OFF THIS DISTRICT'S FIGURE AND NOT TWO DAYS ON IT.**

## The woman of about thirty-one who keeps a route for people who are not in any book

**SHE CAME DOWN FOUR MILES ON THE NINTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH AND WENT BACK UP THEM AN HOUR LATER AND IS IN THIS DISTRICT ON THAT ONE AFTERNOON AND NO OTHER.** 289. **HE ASKED HER FOR ONE THING IN A LANE AND SHE REFUSED IT IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS AND GAVE HIM A REASON SHE HAD NEVER GIVEN ANYBODY — THE ELEVEN YEARS ARE HOW LONG SHE HAS WALKED HER LINES, NOT HOW LONG THE REASON HAS BEEN WAITING, AND AN EARLIER DRAFT OF THIS LINE MADE THE REASON ELEVEN YEARS OLD, WHICH THE PROSE DOES NOT SAY: You have asked me for this three times since the first of last month and I have said no three times and I have never given you a reason, and I am going to give you one now because you have finally asked me for one thing instead of a general favour. I keep lines. I walk them at night in fours and fives and there is a wire on the left of one of them that has taken the skin off a shin twice. If I tell you who is standing at a table, then in eleven years somebody can work out who walks a line. I am not going to stand in a lane and hand you the second one dressed up as the first.** **HER EXIT CLAUSE IS NOT SPENT, HER THREE EARLIER REFUSALS WERE NOT EXTENDED BEYOND THAT ONE, AND NOBODY IN THAT LANE AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM WAS GIVEN A SENTENCE THAT SETTLES ANYTHING, AND HE SAID OUT LOUD IN THE SAME LANE THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO ASK HER A FOURTH TIME AND IS NOT GOING TO FIND ANOTHER WAY ROUND HER.** She is not a bearer of a route, is not a keeper, and has never been asked to be one.

A man of about thirty at a counter, at the sixteenth time down the eleven mile road and the next the seventeenth, and no chapter of this block gave him a day. A man of about forty-one who keeps a road house at the top of the road that climbs, who has no day in this district and this block gave him none. The man with the portfolio.**

# Volume 06 Batch 0005 — Character state after Chapters 291 to 300

**Additive to every section above. Volume 06 is open. The figures below are the state at Chapter 300 and the Volume 06 close inherits them. No person is named in this canon and none is named here.**

**ADDED BY THE REVIEW REPAIR. NO PERSON IN THIS BLOCK CHANGED DESCRIPTOR, AGE, TRADE, RELATIONSHIP OR POSITION, AND NOBODY WENT INTO OR OUT OF A CHAPTER.**

- **The repair put four people into scenes they were already in, and one unnamed person into a question.** A man at the far end of a trestle table in Chapter 300 asked the yard whether anybody had been paid and a woman by the gate answered him; **a woman asking at the bank, about four people on a wall, about four people reading a sheet, and about six people who could not hear the top of a bank are yard and lane texture and are not new characters and are entered nowhere.**
- **The man of about thirty-four who digs loam raises his arm at about half past four in the afternoon in Chapter 291 and not at half past five in the evening, and the question the man of about thirty-seven asks him follows it at about a quarter past five.** Nothing about either man changed but the order of two clocks.
- **The two men on the top of the bank in Chapter 298 are there at about half past two and not half past four, and the man of fifty-six and the man of about thirty-four who mends fencing say and do exactly what they said and did.**
- **The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing read the second of the five lines out loud three more times across the block, in 293, 294 and 296 and again in 300, and was asked what the word *place* meant and did not give it a meaning.** He is still a man of about thirty-four, he is still not a founder and not a keeper of anything, and **he still has not sat in the chair at the end of that trestle table, and Chapter 296's beat is untouched.**
- **The man of fifty-eight, the woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale, the woman of about thirty-four who keeps a goat, the man of about twenty-three who copies for nothing, the man of about twenty-nine who drives a cart, the man of about thirty-seven and the man of about thirty-four who keeps a road are each in the chapters the canon card puts them in and in no others, and the repair changed no chapter's cast.**
- **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO KEEPS A ROUTE IS STILL NOT IN ONE CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK AND A CLERK ENTERED ON THE TWENTIETH THAT NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT SAID ONE WORD ABOUT HER IN ANY OF THE TEN DAYS. HER EXIT CLAUSE IS UNSPENT AND WAS NOT APPROACHED, ASKED FOR, HINTED AT, PREPARED FOR OR FORESHADOWED, AND THAT IS THE THREAD THE UNMET CLIMAX NEEDS.**

## The man the figure of twenty-one years is against

**HE DID NOT GO TO A DOOR FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AWAY ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS AND HE SENT NOBODY TO IT, AND HE SAYS OUT LOUD IN A YARD ON THE FOURTEENTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH THAT SHE TOLD HIM ON THE EIGHTH NOT TO COME AND ASK HER AGAIN THIS MONTH, THAT HE SAID HE WOULD NOT, AND THAT IT WAS A MONTH AND NOT A THING.** 294. **HE WALKED TWO MILES TO A DOOR WITH NOTHING OVER IT ON THE FIFTEENTH AND STOOD IN THE LANE OUTSIDE IT ABOUT NINE MINUTES AND DID NOT GO IN, AND HE WAS SICK BEHIND THE BANK ON THE WAY BACK AND A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING WAS NINE FEET OFF HIM AND SAID NOTHING, AND THE ONLY REASON A CLERK KNOWS ABOUT IT IS THAT HE WAS TOLD ONE SENTENCE.** 295. **ON THE SEVENTEENTH HE SAID THE WHOLE OF THE POSITION OUT LOUD IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE: THAT THE ONLY RECORD OF THAT PLACE IS A BOOK FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AWAY, THAT ONE PERSON MAY WRITE IN IT, THAT SHE HAS NOT BEEN ASKED, AND THAT BEFORE THE FIRST OF THE EIGHTH MONTH SOMEBODY IS GOING TO WANT A THING IN IT AND THERE IS NOBODY BUT HER.** 297. **HE IS A CONVENER AND HE IS FINDABLE AND A NAME IN A COLUMN CANNOT BE TAKEN OFF IT BY THE PERSON IT IS PUT ON. HE IS NOT A FOUNDER, NOT A HOLDER, NOT A BEARER OF A TOLL AND NOT A PARTY OF ANYTHING. HIS OFFER OF THE EIGHTH OF THE SEVENTH WAS NOT WITHDRAWN AND WAS NOT RESOLVED IN THE PREVIOUS BLOCK AND IS NOT RESOLVED IN THIS ONE. THE ROMANCE DID NOT MOVE IN ANY OF THE TEN DAYS OF THIS BLOCK.**

## The registrar of this district's records office

**SHE CAME UP THE BANK ON THE TWELFTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH BECAUSE ABOUT NINE PEOPLE HAD COME OUT OF THE YARD AND WERE STANDING IN THE LANE OUTSIDE HER DOOR, AND SHE READ THE FIVE LINES OF PRINT FROM ABOUT NINE FEET WITHOUT BEING HANDED THEM, AND SAID ONE THING NOBODY ASKED HER FOR: THAT IN NINE YEARS NOT ONE LINE HAS COME INTO THAT BOOK BECAUSE OF SOMETHING SHE DID, THAT THERE IS GOING TO BE ONE, THAT SHE KNOWS WHAT IT SAYS AND WHERE IT IS AIMED, THAT SHE IS NOT GOING TO BE ASKED ABOUT IT, AND THAT SHE HAS NOT READ THE OFFER AND THAT THAT IS NOT A REFUSAL.** 292. **SHE IS NOT ASKED ANYTHING ELSE IN ANY OF THE TEN DAYS, AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK ASKS HER FOR ANYTHING, AND HER REFUSAL OF THE EIGHTH OF THIS MONTH STANDS IN THE RECORD AND WAS KEPT TO. THE SECOND BOOK ON THAT SHELF HAS BEEN OPEN SINCE THE SIXTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND WAS NOT READ IN THIS BLOCK AND NOTHING WAS ENTERED IN IT. SHE IS NOT A BEARER, NOT A HOLDER, NOT A STEWARD AND NOT A PARTY OF ANYTHING, AND A KEEPER OF A ROOM IS NOT A THING SHE IS.**

## The man of about thirty-seven who puts tables up for anybody who will use them

**HE SAID BEFORE ANYBODY ASKED HIM ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH THAT HE CANNOT SET HIS OWN TABLE UP AND HAS NOT BEEN ABLE TO SINCE THE TENTH, AND THAT HE IS SAYING IT IN A YARD AT TEN IN THE MORNING INSTEAD OF AT A WALL ELEVEN MILES OFF AT EIGHT BECAUSE A PERSON IS TOLD THINGS AT A WALL IN THE MORNING A GOOD DEAL WORSE THAN THEY ARE TOLD IN A YARD.** 291. **HE SAID HE IS NOT GOING TO TAKE THAT TABLE DOWN, ON THE TWELFTH AND AGAIN ON THE SIXTEENTH, AND THAT IF HE DID THEN A BODY FOUR HUNDRED MILES OFF WOULD HAVE PRINTED THAT A YARD IS A PLACE WHERE AN OFFER IS MADE AND THERE WOULD BE NOTHING STANDING AT IT.** 292, 296. **HE SAID A TENT IS NOT A LEAN-TO AND THAT HE WOULD RATHER THE FIRST OF HIS FIVE LINES STAYED WRONG ON ITS FACE THAN HAVE HIM MAKE IT TRUE SIDEWAYS, AND ON THE NINETEENTH HE SAID HE IS GOING TO PUT THE TENT UP AGAINST THAT WALL.** 296, 299. **THE TENT NEVER GOES UP.** He gave no name to anybody in this block and was not asked for one. **HE IS NOT A KEEPER OF A TABLE AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE A KEEPER OF ANYTHING, HE IS NOT A PARTY OF ANYTHING, AND HE IS THE SHAPE THE VOLUME'S METHOD IS WEARING AND IS NOT A NEW FINAL ENEMY.**

## The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing

**HE READ THE FIVE LINES OF PRINT OUT IN THAT YARD ON THE TWELFTH AND READ THEM AGAIN ON THE SIXTEENTH, AND ON THE SIXTEENTH HE SAT DOWN ON THE END OF THE TRESTLE TABLE IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES WITH THE CHAIR AT THE END OF THAT TABLE STANDING EIGHTY-FOUR DAYS UNSAT IN, AND SAID IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS THAT HE SAT ON THE TABLE AND NOT IN THE CHAIR BECAUSE HE IS NOT GOING TO BE THE FIRST MAN TO SIT IN THAT CHAIR IN THIS DISTRICT, AND IT WAS NOT COUNTED.** 296. **HE STOOD AT THE TOP OF THE BANK AT ABOUT HALF PAST FOUR ON THE FIFTEENTH AND ON THE EIGHTEENTH AND SAID A THING ON EACH OCCASION THAT HE SAID HE WOULD RATHER HAVE SAID UP THERE THAN DOWN IN THE YARD.** 295, 298. **HE STOPPED IN THE MIDDLE OF A SENTENCE IN A YARD ON THE NINETEENTH, ON PURPOSE, SIX DAYS AFTER A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM STOPPED IN THE MIDDLE OF A SENTENCE IN THE SAME YARD, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A SHAPE NOTICED A SECOND TIME IS NOT A FINDING AND THAT A MAN WHO STOPS TWICE IN SIX DAYS IS NOT MAKING A HABIT OF IT AND HAS NOT BEEN ASKED TO STOP.** 299. **HE IS NOT A FOUNDER AND IS NOT A KEEPER OF ANYTHING AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE ONE.**

## The man of about thirty-four who digs loam

**HE CARRIED A TRESTLE BOARD ELEVEN MILES ON HIS OWN SHOULDER ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH AND COULD NOT GET IT DOWN OFF THE SHOULDER AT THE FAR END, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN GOT UNDER THE OTHER END OF IT WITH BOTH FOREARMS, AND IT CAME DOWN BETWEEN THEM, AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT FOR ABOUT NINE SECONDS.** 291. **HIS HAND HAS NOT CLOSED SINCE THE ELEVENTH OF LAST MONTH AND HIS ARM HAS NOT GONE ABOVE THE LEVEL OF THAT SHOULDER SINCE THE FIRST OF THIS MONTH, AND ON THE TWENTIETH HE SAID IT HAS NOT GONE ABOVE ON ANY OF THE TWENTY DAYS OF THAT MONTH, AND THAT HE CARRIED THAT TABLE OUT FOUR TIMES IN THE MONTH AND CANNOT DO IT ON THE TWENTY-FIRST OR THE TWENTY-SECOND, AND THAT HE HAS NOT TOLD THE MAN IT BELONGS TO.** 291, 300. **HE SAID HALF OF THE BLOCK'S CENTRAL SENTENCE IN A YARD ON THE THIRTEENTH AND STOPPED, AND A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING SAID THE OTHER HALF OF A SENTENCE AND STOPPED ON THE NINETEENTH, AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID THE REST.** 293, 299. **HE STOOD AT THE TOP OF THE DITCH BEHIND THE BUILDING WITH TWO DOORS FOR ABOUT A MINUTE ON THE SIXTEENTH AND DID NOT GO DOWN INTO IT, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM WHETHER HE THINKS THAT BUILDING IS GOING TO BE SHUT.** 296. **HE IS NOT ONE OF THE FIVE AND DOES NOT LIVE IN ANY OF THEIR ROOMS AND IS NOT A CLERK OF A HOUSE AND IS NOT A KEEPER OF ANYTHING.**

## The man of about thirty-four who keeps a road

**HE SAID IN A YARD ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH THAT A SIXTH MORNING AT THAT TABLE IS A FIGURE NOBODY HAS ASKED FOR AND A SEVENTH IS A HABIT, AND THAT HE WAS NOT GOING TO BE THE MAN WHO CAME BACK AND SAID WHICH OF THE TWO THE SEVENTH ONE WAS — AND HE NAMED THE EIGHTEENTH OF THAT MONTH, AND HE CAME BACK ON THE EIGHTEENTH AND SAID IT WAS A HABIT, AND SAID HE HAD SAT IN THE DITCH AT THE FAR END OF THAT FIELD AT ABOUT HALF PAST EIGHT AND COULD NOT GET UP THE FIRST TIME, AND THAT A HABIT AND A FIGURE ARE THE SAME THING AFTER A MONTH AND THAT HE LOOKED FOR A TEST FOR SIX DAYS AND DID NOT FIND ONE.** 291, 298. **HE SAID ON THE NINETEENTH THAT A FORTNIGHT IS A NUMBER AND HE KNEW IT AND ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE IN THAT YARD DID NOT, AND THAT HE DOES NOT KNOW WHICH OF THE TWO HE HAS BEEN TALKING ABOUT FOR A FORTNIGHT.** 299. **HE IS NOT A BEARER OF THE ROAD AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE ONE.**

## The man of fifty-six

**HE READ THE BOARD OUT OVER THE MENDER'S SHOULDER AND GOT THE OTHER TWO FIGURES RIGHT ON ALL TEN DAYS.** 291–300. **HE SAID THAT A HALF-SENTENCE THAT STOPS IN A YARD IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE IS THE ONLY THING ANYBODY HAS DONE ABOUT A FIGURE IN SIX WEEKS THAT HE HAS NOT GOT A BETTER VERSION OF, AND THAT A MAN WHO SAYS THE TRUE THING BY ACCIDENT IN THE WRONG WEEK HAS STILL SAID IT.** 293, 297. **HE SAID THAT A DOCUMENT WHICH SAYS WHAT IT DOES NOT KNOW IS HONEST AND STOPS SOMEBODY, AND THAT A MAN WHO SAYS HE CANNOT TELL A HABIT FROM A FIGURE IS ALSO HONEST AND STOPS NOBODY AT ALL, BECAUSE THERE IS NOTHING IN IT TO STOP ANYTHING.** 298. He is not a clerk and has said so about himself about a dozen times in four months.

NO CASE AND NO PORTFOLIO AND NO LIST AND NO SEAL AND NO PAPER, BOTH HANDS DOWN AT HIS SIDES THE WHOLE WAY FROM THE GATE TO THE TABLE, ABOUT FIVE FOOT NINE, GREY AT THE TEMPLES, THIN ACROSS THE TOP OF THE SHOULDERS, THE OUTSIDE EDGE OF THE SOLE OF HIS RIGHT SHOE GONE, THE HEEL OF HIS LEFT ONE OFF IN A PIECE ABOUT THE SIZE OF A COIN, AND A PALE PRINT ABOUT NINE INCHES LONG UNDER EVERY STEP ON THE DRY PART OF THAT YARD, AND HE DID NOT LOOK DOWN AT ONE OF THEM.** He gave no name to anybody and nobody asked him for one, and the record about the not asking says not asked. 301.

**HE CAME BECAUSE HE HEARD A BELL. HE WAS IN THE SOUND OF IT ON THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AT ABOUT TWO IN THE MORNING, ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED MILES AWAY, FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES, AND HE DID NOT GO TO A DOOR AND DID NOT GO TO A BUILDING AND STOOD WHERE HE WAS. 309. **HE SAID HE HAD COME NINE DAYS ON HIS OWN TWO FEET, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT NINE IS A FIGURE ABOUT HIS OWN LEGS AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A ROAD IN THIS DISTRICT AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A DISTANCE, AND THAT NOBODY ASKED HIM WHERE HE STARTED FROM. 301. ## The man of about thirty-four who keeps a road

A man of about forty-four with a case is not in this district, his list is not in this district, nobody has seen it and nobody has counted it, and no chapter of this block has him arrive again or says where he went. **THE QUIET REGISTRY AND THE LOCK COUNCIL ARE BODIES THAT PRINT AND DO NOT ARRIVE, NEITHER IS NAMED IN THIS BLOCK AND NEITHER ACQUIRED A FACE. NO NEW FINAL ENEMY. A KEEPER HAS NEVER BEEN ON A PAGE AND THERE NEVER WAS ONE AND NO KEEPER IS NAMED; THE BARE WORD OCCURS TWENTY-SEVEN TIMES IN THIS BLOCK AND EVERY ONE OF THE TWENTY-SEVEN IS A DENIAL, A PRINTED RULE ABOUT A JOB, A COLUMN THAT IS EMPTY, A LINE THAT A PERSON DOES NOT DO ANYTHING TO, OR A NOUN SOMEBODY HAS BEEN TOLD THEY ARE NOT. THE BELL IS NOT IN ANY CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK AND THE LITERAL STRINGS *A BELL*, *THE BELL* AND *A ROPE* ARE AT ZERO ACROSS IT, AGAINST NINETEEN, SIX AND ONE IN THE BLOCK BEFORE.**

# Volume 07 Batch 0003 — Character State, Chapters 321 to 330

**Additive to every section above. Volume 07 is open. Every entry below is the state at Chapter 330.**

## The man the figure of twenty-one years is against, and the registrar of this district's records office

**HE IS IN SEVEN OF THE TEN CHAPTERS OF THIS BLOCK AND SPEAKS IN SIX, AND HE IS GIVEN AN INTERIOR LIFE FOR THE FIRST TIME IN THE MANUSCRIPT.** He sat in the chair at the end of the trestle table at about a quarter past eleven on the tenth of the eighth month, in the building with two doors, to write a charter, and nobody had sat in that chair in a hundred and nine days, and nobody asked him to. He wrote eight lines in one hand between about half past eleven and about two, and at about two he said out loud, in a hundred and thirty-three words, that he had been wrong about the document since the moment he started it, that he had found the word NOT ASKED out loud on the fourth of the eighth month when a woman asked how many people were in that yard, that stopping a removal is not the same as working, and that he has nobody to ask whether it works and is not going to go and find one. 321.

**HE WAS WRONG FOUR TIMES IN THIS BLOCK, IN SCENE, AND THE TEXT MAKES EACH OF THEM PLAIN.** That a document with nobody in it is more honest than one with somebody in it. 323. That a building with a door, a table and a chair is a shelter. 322. That a shape is different because it is his. 323. That there is a way to find out whether the roll works. 328.

**HE ASKED ONE QUESTION IN THIS BLOCK THAT A PERSON COULD HAVE REFUSED** — at a table with four legs outside a door, on the eleventh of the eighth month, whether a man of about thirty-eight who cuts reeds would come into that building at two in the morning if it went off — **and the man said no in about four seconds with a reason of his own and called it a shed with a door on it, and he wanted the yes and caught himself wanting it about two seconds after it had not come, and he said out loud in daylight that he was wrong and was not going to go round the man. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO HAS WANTED AN ANSWER AND GOT ONE IS NOT A MAN WHO WAS REPRESENTED BY THE ANSWER.** 322.

**HE PAID FOR IT IN HIS BODY IN FOUR CHAPTERS AND NOT IN A FOOTER.** A chair carried into a building and back out. Five hours of writing, and after about half past two on the tenth he could not hold the pen upright in his right hand at all and ruled the second line with a straight edge braced against his forearm and got it about a sixteenth of an inch out of true and left it. His right hand would not close properly from about half past two on the tenth, and the clerk of nineteen years had to open it with her fingers one at a time on the eleventh. Two buckets of water carried in from the top of that lane in two trips on the eleventh, because the right hand would not hold the handle of a full one. The second of the two terms written in his left hand on the thirteenth. No writing at all on the fourteenth, and the clerk of nineteen years came down the lane and wrote what he said in her own hand and he did not read it back. A sheet of print he could not lift off a bench with his right hand on the sixteenth. The two sheets laid out on the table with four legs on the nineteenth in his left hand alone.

**HIS HAND IS NOT THE FENCER'S LEFT HAND AND NOT THE HAND OF THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE WHO COPIES FOR NOTHING AND NOT THE ARM OF THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT FOUR HANDS AND AN ARM IN THIS DISTRICT ARE FIVE DIFFERENT THINGS AND ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER.** 322.

**HE HAD THE VOLUME'S REVERSAL IN HIS HAND AT ABOUT HALF PAST ELEVEN ON THE FIFTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH AND DID NOT MAKE IT OUT OF IT, AND HE SAID WHAT HE HAD OUT LOUD AT ABOUT FOUR IN A HUNDRED AND NINETY-FIVE WORDS SO THAT IT WOULD BE ON A PAGE AND NOT ONLY IN HIM: THAT THERE IS A LIST BIGGER THAN FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN, THAT EVERY NAME IN A RECORD OF WHO WAS IN THE SOUND OF THAT BELL IS THE NAME OF SOMEBODY WHO DID NOT COME TO A DOOR, THAT A NAME SAID OUT LOAD IS A CONSENT THE PERSON IT BELONGS TO DID NOT GIVE, THAT HE CANNOT RECOMMEND IT AND CANNOT REFUSE IT AND IS NOT THE ONE HOLDING IT, THAT A NUMBER MADE BY KNOCKING, A NUMBER MADE BY WALKING AND A NUMBER MADE BY LISTENING ARE THREE NUMBERS AND ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO MAKE THE FINDING AND IS NOT GOING TO STOP ANYBODY MAKING IT. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A CLERK WHO COULD HAVE MADE A FINDING OUT OF TWO FIGURES AND DID NOT IS NOT A CLERK WHO HAS FOUND NOTHING.** 326.

**HE PUT HIS OWN NAME ON A RULED LINE AT THE FOOT OF THAT ROLL AT ABOUT HALF PAST THREE ON THE SEVENTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH, IN CHALK, WITH HIS LEFT HAND, AND THE NAME IS NOT PRINTED IN ANY CHAPTER AND NO CHAPTER ENTERS IT ANYWHERE.** 328. **HE STOOD ON THE STEP OF TWO STONES OUTSIDE THAT ROOM AT ABOUT FIVE ON the seventeenth with the five lines of print in his coat and did not pick it up and did not knock, and he understood, standing on that step, that a document a stranger may walk up to and read is worth more to him than a document in a room, and that a registrar who would rather have a document left on a step outside a shut door than carried into a room four hundred yards from a gate is a man whose whole trade has been turned over in a fortnight.** 328.

**HE ASKED FOR NOTHING OF ANYBODY IN THE TEN DAYS AND SENT NOBODY. ON THE NINETEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE BOOKS CAME DOWN THE BANK AND STOOD NINE FEET OFF THAT TABLE, WHICH IS THE DISTANCE HE STANDS AT, AND HE ASKED HER NOTHING, ASKED NOBODY ELSE TO ASK HER NOTHING, DID NOT GO AFTER HER AND SENT NOBODY, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE ASKED FOR NOTHING. HE SAID OUT LOUD THAT SHE IS NOT A FAVOUR AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO WRITE IT DOWN AS ONE.** 330. **HE IS STILL NOT NAMED. THE BATCH 0003 PROMPT'S DEFAULT WAS TO KEEP THE VOLUME'S NO-NAMES RULE AND THAT DEFAULT WAS TAKEN AND IS DISCLOSED, AND THE PROPOSED CANON CHANGE IS UNSETTLED.**

## The woman of about thirty-one who keeps a route for people who are not in any book

**SHE IS IN ONE CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK AND IT IS THE CHAPTER THAT SPENDS HER ELEVEN WORDS.** She is not a new person. 325.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SIX ASKED HER WHETHER SHE WOULD PUT HER NAME UNDER THE WORD NOT ASKED IN THE ROLL, AND SHE SAID NO IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A REASON OF HER OWN, IN A HUNDRED AND SIXTY-EIGHT WORDS: IF I PUT MY NAME UNDER THAT WORD, THEN I AM IN A COLUMN, AND THE COLUMN IS IN A BOOK, AND IN NINE YEARS SOMEBODY WHO HAS NEVER MET ME WILL READ THAT I WAS ASKED AND I SAID NO, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF WHAT IS LEFT OF ME, AND IT IS A LINE WITH MY NAME ON IT AND A RULE UNDER IT, AND A PIECE OF PAPER IS THE EASY WAY AND I AM NOT GOING TO TAKE IT BECAUSE A MAN IN THAT YARD HAS BEEN CAREFUL.** 325.

**AT ABOUT FOUR IN THE AFTERNOON SHE WENT INTO A ROOM ABOUT NINE FEET BY NINE AT THE TOP OF THAT LANE, PAST THE THIRD HOUSE FROM THE SCALE, WITH NOTHING IN HER HANDS, AND SAID BEFORE HE HAD THE DOOR SHUT THAT SHE WAS BRINGING NOTHING IN AND SAID IT ONCE. NOTHING CAME OUT OF THAT ROOM IN HER HANDS AND NOBODY WROTE ANYTHING DOWN IN IT.** 325.

**SHE CHOSE THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING OUT OF THREE PEOPLE IN THIS DISTRICT SHE HAD REASON TO SAY IT TO — THE MAN WHO READS THINGS OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD, THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SIX, AND THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST — AND THE CHOOSING IS IN A SCENE.** 325.

**SHE GAVE ELEVEN WORDS, ONCE, IN THAT ROOM. THEY ARE NOT ON THE PAGE.** They carry no name, no place, no figure and no body; they are a condition and not a promise; they do not say what she will do. She did not say them a second time to anybody and nobody asked her to. **The only figure on the page is eleven, and eleven is a figure about eleven words and is not a figure about a person.** 325. **THE ONLY FIGURE IN FIVE HUNDRED AND THIRTY CHAPTERS THAT IS ABOUT SOMETHING SHE SAID AND NOTHING ELSE.**

**SHE IS A WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE AND IS NOT THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A ROOM WITH NOTHING OVER THE DOOR, NOT THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO KEEPS A SCALE, NOT THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A GOAT AND NOT THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT, AND ALL FIVE ARE SEPARATED IN THE ONE PARAGRAPH IN WHICH SHE IS NAMED IN THIS BLOCK. SHE HAS A CUT ABOUT THE LENGTH OF TWO FINGERS ON THE INSIDE OF HER LEFT SHIN AND PUTS HER WEIGHT ON THE OUTSIDE OF THAT BOOT, AND SHE CAME IN BY THE GAP IN THE WALL ABOUT FOUR FEET WIDE AND WENT ROUND THE DITCH AT THE END OF THE BANK.** 325. **SHE IS NOT A CLERK OF A HOUSE, NOT A KEEPER OF A ROAD, NOT A KEEPER OF A ROLL AND NOT A KEEPER OF THE ELEVEN WORDS, AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE A KEEPER OF ANYTHING. HER THREE REFUSALS IN THIS VOLUME ARE THREE REFUSALS ON THREE DAYS OF THREE DIFFERENT THINGS ASKED OF ONE PERSON BY THREE DIFFERENT MEN, AND THREE IS A FIGURE ABOUT THREE REFUSALS AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT HER.**

## The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing

**HE ASKED THE QUESTION THE BLOCK IS BUILT OUT OF**, in a hundred and five words, on the tenth of the eighth month: where is the person who is in the roll and not in it at the same time. 321. **HE READ THE TWO TERMS OUT LOUD IN THE ORDINARY VOICE TO ABOUT NINE PEOPLE WITHOUT BEING HANDED THE SHEET AND ASKED WHICH ONE OF THEM IT IS FOR, AND SAID HE WAS ASKING FOR THE FINGER AND NOT FOR THE NAME, IN A HUNDRED AND NINE WORDS.** 324. **HE ASKED THE QUESTION OF THE DOCUMENT, IN eighty-four words, on the seventeenth: how does anybody in this basin find out, on some morning in six months, whether that sheet did the thing.** 328. **HE READ ALL EIGHT LINES AND THE NAME AT THE FOOT OF THEM AND THE TWO TERMS OUT FROM THE TOP, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO DRIVES A CART READ IT BACK FROM THE TOP AND GOT NOTHING WRONG.** 329. **HE SAID IN A HUNDRED AND SIXTY-FIVE WORDS THAT A THING THAT SAYS A PERSON LEAVES IT WHEN THEY GO IS NOT A PROMISE AND NOT A DOOR AND NOT A RULE BUT THE ABSENCE OF A RULE, AND THAT IT IS NOT A CAGE, AND THAT HE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT IT IS AND IS NOT GOING TO GIVE IT A NAME SO THE REST OF A YARD FEEL BETTER ABOUT IT.** 329.

**HE IS THE MAN SHE CHOSE, AND HE SAID IN A HUNDRED AND THIRTEEN WORDS IN THAT YARD AT ABOUT SIX ON THE FOURTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH THAT SHE SAID ELEVEN WORDS TO HIM IN A ROOM, THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO SAY WHAT THEY WERE AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD IS GOING TO MAKE HIM, THAT IT WAS A CONDITION AND NOT A PROMISE AND WAS NOT ABOUT HER AND WAS NOT ABOUT WHAT SHE IS GOING TO DO, AND THAT IF ANYBODY IN THAT YARD WANTS THE WORDS THEY CAN ASK HER.** 325. **HE DID NOT ASK HER. HE ASKED HER TWO THINGS IN BATCH 0002 AND WAS REFUSED TWICE AND HE DID NOT ASK HER A THIRD TIME IN THIS BLOCK.**

**HE WENT UP THE BANK AT ABOUT HALF PAST FOUR ON THE TWELFTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH, STOOD AT THE STEP OF TWO STONES, PUT HIS HAND ON THE WOOD BESIDE THE SHEET OF PRINT THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR LAID THERE ON THE NINTH, TOOK IT AWAY AGAIN, AND DID NOT KNOCK.** 323. **HE IS A MAN WHO READS THINGS OUT LOUD AND IS NOT A FOUNDER, NOT A KEEPER, NOT A BEARER, NOT A COURIER AND NOT A PARTY OF ANYTHING, AND HE HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE ONE.**

## The man of about fifty-six

**HE READ THE BOARD OUT ON ALL TEN DAYS OF THIS BLOCK AND GOT THE OTHER TWO FIGURES RIGHT EVERY TIME.** 321 TO 330. **HE ASKED THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO KEEPS A ROUTE WHETHER SHE WOULD PUT HER NAME UNDER THE WORD NOT ASKED IN THE ROLL, AND SHE SAID NO IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS.** 325. **HE SAID IN A HUNDRED AND SIXTEEN WORDS THAT HE HAS BEEN IN THAT YARD FIVE MONTHS SAYING THAT A DOOR IS NOT A KEEPER AND A WALL IS NOT AN ADDRESS, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO PUT TWO FIGURES TOGETHER THAT SOMEBODY ELSE WALKED OUT, AND THAT NOBODY PUT ONE OF THEM THERE, AND THAT HE HAS NOT GOT AN ARGUMENT AND IS NOT GOING TO HAVE ONE TODAY.** 326. **HE WAS TOLD BY THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING THAT THE ROLL HAD BEEN SIGNED AND HE IS THE ONE PERSON HE TOLD, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A THING HEARD BY TWO PEOPLE IS NOT A RECORD AND IS NOT A DOCUMENT.** 328. **HE IS NOT A CLERK AND HAS SAID SO ABOUT HIMSELF ABOUT A DOZEN TIMES IN FOUR MONTHS, AND HE WAS NOT ASKED WHETHER THAT BELL SHOULD BE PULLED ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS AND HE IS NOT ASKED ABOUT THE FORTY-FOURTH MORNING OF THAT TABLE ELEVEN MILES OFF.**

## The man of about thirty-four who digs loam

**HIS ARM HAS NOT GONE ABOVE THE LEVEL OF THAT SHOULDER SINCE THE FIRST OF THE SEVENTH MONTH AND IT IS NOT SPENT AGAIN IN THIS BLOCK, AND THAT IS DISCLOSED RATHER THAN SMOOTHED OVER.** 327. **HE SAID IN A HUNDRED AND THIRTY-EIGHT WORDS THAT HE IS NOT SAYING THE SIXTH SHAPE IS WORSE THAN THE OTHER FIVE, AND THAT THE WORD OVER HIS IS NOT ASKED AND THE WORD OVER THE ONE ON THAT GATEPOST IS A JOB NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS, AND THAT HE WOULD PUT THEM IN THE SAME DRAWER, AND THAT HE HAS GOT A BAD ARM AND IS NOT GOING TO MAKE IT INTO AN ARGUMENT ABOUT HANDWRITING.** 323. **HE ANSWERED THE WORD PRACTISE IN A HUNDRED AND FORTY-TWO WORDS: YOU CANNOT PRACTISE A THING WITH PEOPLE WHO HAVE NOT BEEN ASKED, BECAUSE A PRACTICE OF A THING IS A THING DONE BY PEOPLE WHO AGREED TO DO IT, AND IF YOU ASK THEM THEN IT IS NOT A PRACTICE, IT IS AN INSTRUCTION, AND AN INSTRUCTION IS A THING THAT GOES OUT TO PEOPLE WHO DID NOT AGREE TO IT, AND HE HAS BEEN IN NINE THINGS IN THIS BASIN THAT HE DID NOT AGREE TO AND IS NOT GOING TO BE IN A TENTH ONE DRESSED UP AS A REHEARSAL.** 327. **HE CAME UP THAT LANE AT ABOUT FIVE ON THE SEVENTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH AND STOOD AT THE END OF THAT TABLE AND SAID NOTHING AND WENT BACK DOWN.** 328. **HE IS NOT ONE OF THE FIVE AND DOES NOT LIVE IN ANY OF THEIR ROOMS AND IS NOT A CLERK OF A HOUSE AND IS NOT A KEEPER OF ANYTHING. HE HAS STILL NOT TOLD THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN THAT THE TABLE ELEVEN MILES OFF IS HIS.**

## The man of about thirty-eight who cuts reeds — **and he is the one person new in this block**

**HE IS BROUGHT IN AT CHAPTER 322, IN THE SAME PARAGRAPH AS HIS TRADE, HIS HOOK, HIS BUNDLE ABOUT NINE INCHES LONG OVER HIS SHOULDER AND THE LANE, AND HE IS IN NO OTHER CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK.** He had not been asked anything by anybody in that lane before that morning and the record about the not asking says not asked. **HE GAVE NO NAME AND NOBODY ASKED HIM FOR ONE.** 322.

**HE SAID NO IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A REASON OF HIS OWN, IN A HUNDRED AND FORTY-SEVEN WORDS, TO THE QUESTION WHETHER HE WOULD COME INTO THAT BUILDING AT TWO IN THE MORNING IF IT WENT OFF: I HAVE GOT NO BOOTS THAT WILL GO OVER THAT DITCH IN THE DARK AND THAT DITCH IS ABOUT THREE FEET DEEP WITH ABOUT A FOOT OF STANDING WATER IN IT, AND I HAVE LIVED ON THE EDGE OF IT FOR SIX YEARS, AND YOU HAVE NOT ASKED ME TO COME, AND I AM NOT GOING TO BE ONE OF THE MEN WHO STANDS IN THAT WATER AT TWO IN THE MORNING BECAUSE A MAN ASKED ME AT A TABLE AT ELEVEN, AND YOU HAVE A TABLE AND A CHAIR AND A DOOR AND A CURTAIN ON A NAIL AND YOU HAVE NOT GOT A STOVE AND YOU HAVE NOT GOT A FLOOR I HAVE SEEN AND YOU HAVE NOT GOT WATER, AND THAT IS A SHED WITH A DOOR ON IT AND IT IS A GOOD SHED AND I AM NOT STANDING IN IT TELLING ANYBODY IT IS A HOUSE.** 322.

**A CLERK ENTERED THAT A REFUSAL WITH A REASON IS NOT ONE OF THE REFUSALS WITH NO REASON A CLERK OF A HOUSE HAS GIVEN, AND THAT A MAN WHO CUTS REEDS IS NOT A CLERK OF A HOUSE AND IS NOT A KEEPER OF A DITCH AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE A KEEPER OF ANYTHING, AND THAT A SHED WITH A DOOR ON IT IS NOT A SHELTER AND A SHELTER IS NOT A SHED.** 322. **HIS NO IS NOT A TENTH REFUSAL OF ELEVEN COPPERS A WEEK, NOT A NINTH REFUSAL WITH NO REASON AND NOT AN EIGHTH ABOUT THE NINTH HOLDING, AND HE IS NOT ON ANY PAGE AND HAS NOT BEEN ASKED FOR ANYTHING ELSE.**

## The man of about thirty-seven who puts tables up for anybody who will use them

**HE CAME UP THAT LANE AT ABOUT TWO IN THE AFTERNOON ON THE FOURTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH WITH NOTHING IN HIS HANDS, WHICH IS NOT WHAT HE USUALLY BRINGS, AND STOOD AT THE TABLE WITH FOUR LEGS AGAINST THE OUTSIDE WALL OF THAT BUILDING FOR ABOUT FORTY MINUTES AND LOOKED AT THE TWO LINES OF PRINT ON IT WITHOUT GOING INTO THE BUILDING, AND SAID ONE THING OUT LOAD TO ABOUT NINE PEOPLE WHICH WAS NOT COUNTED AND WAS THAT A SHEET OF PRINT THAT IS OUT WHERE A PERSON WALKS PAST IT IS WORTH MORE THAN A SHEET OF PRINT THAT IS IN A ROOM, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO BE THE MAN WHO SAYS WHICH OF THOSE TWO IS RIGHT IN FRONT OF A YARD.** 325. **HE WAS AT THE FOOT OF THE EAST WALL OF THAT YARD ON the fourteenth, the eighteenth and the nineteenth with his hand-cart against it and the tent still rolled on the back of it, AND THE TENT HAS NOT GONE UP ON ANY DAY SINCE THE THIRTIETH OF THE SIXTH MONTH.** 322, 325, 327, 329, 330. **HE IS NOT A KEEPER OF A TABLE AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE A KEEPER OF ANYTHING, AND HE GAVE NO NAME TO ANYBODY AND WAS NOT ASKED FOR ONE.**

**HE HAS BEEN IN THIS DISTRICT TWENTY DAYS AT CHAPTER 321 AND TWENTY-NINE AT CHAPTER 330, COUNTED WITHOUT THE DAY HE ARRIVED ON, AND HE HAS SLEPT ON THAT LOW WALL ON TWENTY OF THE TWENTY-ONE NIGHTS OF THAT RUN AT CHAPTER 321 AND TWENTY-NINE OF THE THIRTY AT CHAPTER 330, AND HE HAS NOT SLEPT ON IT ON ONE NIGHT AND NOBODY ASKED HIM ABOUT THE ONE.** 321 TO 330.

**HE WAS IN THAT YARD ON THE FIFTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH FOR ABOUT AN HOUR ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE END OF THAT TRESTLE TABLE AND ABOUT NINE FEET OFF IT FOR THE REST OF THAT DAY, AND THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST STOOD ABOUT NINE FEET OFF HIM FOR ABOUT FORTY MINUTES AT ABOUT ONE O'CLOCK AND DID NOT ASK HIM ONE QUESTION ABOUT THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT ASKING SAYS NOT ASKED AND THAT A MAN WHO CARRIES A MESSAGE FROM ONE PLACE TO ANOTHER ON HIS OWN TWO FEET IS NOT A KEEPER OF IT AND DOES NOT BECOME ONE BY STANDING IN A YARD FOR A DAY.** 326. **HE WAS ASKED NOTHING BY ANYBODY IN THAT YARD ON ANY OF THE TEN days of this block, and a clerk entered that.**

NOT BEFORE, AND NOT IN A YARD, AND THE CHOOSING OF THE PERSON IT IS SAID TO IS A REAL ACT AND IS IN A SCENE.

**SHE IS A WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX AND NOT A MAN, HAS STOOD AT THAT SCALE NINE YEARS, WEIGHS ABOUT NINE SACKS A DAY AND HAS NEVER WEIGHED A DAY, AND HER THREE WORDS — *RATE*, *EVEN* AND *STOCK* — ARE NOT SAID AGAIN IN THIS BLOCK AND ARE NOT EXPLAINED BY HER. ## Volume 07 Batch 0003 — review-fix pass: no character state changed, and the proof

**THE BATCH 0003 REVIEW-FIX PASS EDITED ALL TEN CHAPTERS AND CHANGED NOT ONE CHARACTER. THIS SECTION EXISTS TO SAY THAT PLAINLY, BECAUSE "NOTHING ABOUT A PERSON CHANGED" IS A CLAIM AND THIS IS THE EVIDENCE FOR IT, AND A REPAIR CLAIMED AND NOT MADE IS WORSE THAN NO CLAIM.**

**WHAT WAS EDITED: the wording of the ten chapter openings, the wording of the ten closing ledgers, the wording of the ten all-caps footers, five forms of a clause inside the figures speech, ten redundant ledger tails, and three figure or date slips. EVERY ONE OF THOSE IS FRAME, RESTATEMENT OR ARITHMETIC. NOT ONE OF THEM IS A MOTION, A SPEECH, A GESTURE, A COST, A REFUSAL, A QUESTION, AN ANSWER OR A RELATIONSHIP.**

**WHAT THAT MEANS, PERSON BY PERSON, AND IT MEANS THE SAME THING FOR ALL OF THEM:**

- **THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST / THE REGISTRAR.** Still in seven of the ten chapters, still speaks in six, still costs himself the chair, the pen, the two trips with the buckets, the left-hand signature and the right hand that will not close, and still asks for nothing and sends nobody. He still says *I do not know* in Chapter 321, still says the thing about a shed in Chapter 322, still says the opposite out loud about the drawer in Chapter 323, still says *for nobody* in Chapter 324, still says what he has got and has not got in Chapter 330, and still does not read his own document out loud in Chapter 329. **NOT ONE WORD HE SAYS WAS TOUCHED.**
- **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO KEEPS A ROUTE.** Still refuses in about four seconds in Chapter 325 with her own reason, still chooses one of three, still gives eleven words once, still is not asked twice. Her cut shin and her weight on the outside of her left boot are untouched.
- **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT WHO CUTS REEDS.** Still the one person new to the block, still brought in with his trade, his hook, his bundle and the lane, still says no in about four seconds in Chapter 322, still calls the building a shed with a door on it, still gave no name and was still asked for none. **HIS *NO* IS NOT ONE OF THE THREE REFUSALS GIVEN IN THE YARD OF LOT SEVENTEEN, BECAUSE HE WAS REFUSED AT A TABLE WITH FOUR LEGS AT THE END OF THE LANE AND NOT IN THAT YARD, AND THE REPAIR THAT CORRECTED THE COUNT FROM TWO TO THREE IN CHAPTERS 326 AND 328 DID NOT TOUCH HIM.**
- **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A ROAD, THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SIX, THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR, THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO KEEPS A SCALE, THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT, THE CHILD OF ABOUT EIGHT, THE CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS, THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE BOOKS.** Every speech, every cost, every non-asking and every hour of every one of the ten days is where it was.
- **THE NAME THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR CARRIES.** Still not said, still not on any document, still not repeated by him, still not asked about by anybody.
- **THE ROMANCE.** Did not move. No declaration in either mouth, no settlement, and the man the figure of twenty-one years is against is not named as anything by anybody.

**THE ONE CHARACTER-STATE ITEM THE REPAIR TOUCHED IS NOT A CHARACTER. It is the figure for the three refusals given in the yard of Lot Seventeen in the eighth month, which is now three and was printed as two in Chapters 326 and 328, and that figure is about three refusals and not about the woman who gave them — which is what Chapter 325 has always said it was.**

# Volume 07 Batch 0004 — Character state, Chapters 331 to 340

**Additive to every section above. Volume 07 is written through. Chapter 340 is the last day of the volume. Nothing below is carried forward from another file and nothing in an earlier file was edited by this block, with one exception that is named at the last section.**

## The man the figure of twenty-one years is against, and the registrar of this district's records office

**HE IS IN NINE OF THE TEN CHAPTERS AND SPEAKS IN SEVEN. WHAT THIS BLOCK COST HIM WAS PAID IN HIS BODY, IN FOUR CHAPTERS, AND NOT IN A FOOTER, AND A FOOTER IS NOT THE LAST PLACE HE APPEARS.**

- **On the twentieth** he was at the bank from about ten until the light went, said out loud in a yard that he has a hand that will not close and has not slept in six nights and that if anything is done in that lane at two in the morning it will be done by a person who was asked to do it or by nobody, and asked nobody anything.
- **On the twenty-first** he went up the bank at about half past eight, did not go into that building, went back down and out through the gate, and was not in the district again until about two in the morning. **HE HAD BEEN OUT OF THE DISTRICT FOR THE WHOLE OF THE NIGHT, AND HE WALKED ELEVEN MILES UP A ROAD AND ELEVEN MILES BACK IN THE DARK, AND HE DID NOT HEAR IT.**
- **On the twenty-second** he came out of the gate at about ten past four in the morning into a yard with about nineteen people in it who had been standing in it since about two in the morning. He asked nobody what had happened. **HE STOOD AT THE END OF THAT TRESTLE TABLE FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES BEFORE HE COULD LOOK AT ANYTHING ON IT, BECAUSE THE YARD WAS TOO BRIGHT AFTER THE DARK.**
- **On the twenty-third** he stood about nine feet off the end of that table from about half past ten until about twenty past twelve and **DID NOT READ** the account of the night or the list. The four reasons are entered: a mark may not carry a job; a person standing next to a thing is not standing in its place; he was not in the sound of it because he was asleep in a bed four hundred yards off it; and a document is read out by somebody who was in the thing. **WHAT HE LOSES IS THE READING. IT IS NOT A LIMB AND IT IS NOT HIS LIFE AND HE IS ALIVE AND ABOUT NINE PEOPLE IN THAT YARD SPOKE TO HIM ABOUT THE WEATHER AND THE GATE BEFORE ELEVEN AND ABOUT THE LOAMS AFTER TWELVE, AND THAT IS ENTERED AS SOCIAL RECOGNITION AND NOT AS AUTHORITY.**
- **On the twenty-fourth** he spent the whole day choosing a person and chose the woman of fifty-eight out of four, and did not go down a lane to the woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale and did not put a question to the woman of about thirty-four who keeps a goat, and went up the bank at about three and stood on a step and did not knock and came back down.
- **On the twenty-fifth** he was in a room with a shut door in it at about half past four and a man of about sixty-four said a name in it and he heard it and nothing went into his hands and nothing came out in anybody's hands.
- **On the twenty-seventh** **HIS RIGHT HAND CLOSED ABOUT A STONE ABOUT THE SIZE OF A FIST FOR THE FIRST TIME SINCE THE TENTH OF THIS MONTH**, and he carried it about four inches and set it down on the other corner of two sheets of print, and a clerk of nineteen years was standing about nine feet off him and did not offer to do it and did not say anything about it. **A HAND IS A FIGURE ABOUT A HAND AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A DOCUMENT, AND THE ONE BOOK HE WOULD WANT TO WRITE IN IS THE SECOND OF THE TWO FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THAT GATE AND A HAND THAT CAN HOLD A STONE IS NOT A REASON TO GO UP THAT BANK.**
- **On the twenty-eighth** he stood about nine feet off the end of that table from about ten until about four and did not go down to the low wall once, and said out loud that he cannot price the difference between a number made by knocking and a number made by listening and is going to leave it as a thing this district has no way to price, in public, so that it is a thing and not a silence.
- **On the twenty-ninth** he was told in that yard at about two in the afternoon that there is one line in the second of the two books. **HE DID NOT GO UP THE BANK AND HE DID NOT STAND ON THE STEP OF TWO STONES AND HE DID NOT KNOCK, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE WAS TOLD NOTHING AND ASKED NOTHING AND SENT NOBODY AND THAT HE ASKED FOR NOTHING.**
- **HIS HAND HAS NOT CLOSED PROPERLY SINCE THE TENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH AND IT CLOSED ABOUT A STONE ON THE TWENTY-SEVENTH. IT IS THE ONLY TEMPORARY INJURY IN THE MANUSCRIPT. THE CLOSE MAY FIND IT BETTER AND MAY NOT FIND IT WORSE WITHOUT A SCENE, AND IT IS A FIGURE ABOUT A HAND AND NOT ABOUT A DOCUMENT.**
- **HE ASKED FOR NOTHING OF ANYBODY IN THE TEN DAYS AND SENT NOBODY AND ASKED NOBODY ELSE TO ASK ANYTHING FOR HIM, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE ASKED FOR NOTHING ON FIVE OF THE TEN DAYS. HIS MONTH WAS NOT USED AS A LEVER AND THE EIGHTH OF THE SEVENTH WAS NOT REOPENED AS A WOUND. NEITHER OF THEM SAYS A DECLARATION AND THE BLOCK DOES NOT END ON ONE.**

## The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing

**He tied the knot on the twentieth with the fingers of his right hand, because his left hand has not closed since the eleventh of the sixth month and nine inches of rope cannot be held in a fist for nine minutes. HE HELD THE ROPE ON THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FIRST WITH THE LOOP OF THAT KNOT OVER THE INSIDE OF HIS RIGHT WRIST, AND HE IS THE MAN WHO HELD IT THE FIRST TIME. HE ASKED THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A ROAD ONE QUESTION AND WAS REFUSED, HE ASKED THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST ONE QUESTION IN A YARD ON THE TWENTY-FIFTH AND WAS NOT ANSWERED, HE LENT HIS ROOM FOR TWENTY MINUTES ON THE TWENTY-FIFTH AND DID NOT GO BACK UP THAT LANE, HE READ THE ACCOUNT OF THE NIGHT AND THE LIST OUT ALOUD IN DAYLIGHT ON THE TWENTY-THIRD AND WAS READ BACK, AND HE READ THE ROLL OUT AND HAD IT READ BACK ON THE TWENTY-SEVENTH AND THE TWENTY-NINTH. HE IS THE LAST PERSON ON THE PAGE OF THE LAST DAY OF THE VOLUME, AND WHAT HE IS DOING THERE IS PUTTING HIS LEFT HAND FLAT ON THE OUTSIDE WALL OF A BUILDING WITH TWO DOORS AND TAKING IT OFF AGAIN, AND THE WALL IS WARM, AND THE WALL STAYS WARM. HE IS NOT A FOUNDER, NOT A KEEPER, NOT A BEARER, NOT A COURIER AND NOT A PARTY OF ANYTHING, AND HE HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE ONE AND SAYS SO OUT LOUD IN A YARD IN THIS BLOCK.**

## The man of about sixty-four who carries a message from one place to another on his own two feet

**HE IS IN THIS DISTRICT THIRTY NINE DAYS AT THE LAST DAY OF THE VOLUME AND HE IS STILL HERE, AND HIS STAY AND HIS NIGHTS BOTH CLIMB BY ONE A DAY AND BOTH REPRODUCE: THIRTY DAYS AND THIRTY OF THIRTY-ONE NIGHTS AT CHAPTER 331, THIRTY NINE DAYS AND THIRTY NINE OF FORTY AT CHAPTER 340. THE NIGHT HE DID NOT SLEEP ON THAT WALL IS THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FIRST, THE ONE HE WAS IN THE SOUND ON, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE WAS IN THIS DISTRICT THAT NIGHT AND DID NOT LIE DOWN.** He came in on the morning of the twenty-first of the seventh month with nothing in his hands, after nine days on foot, and gave no name and was never asked for one. **HE HAS NOW LOST THE EAR ON THE LEFT SIDE. IT WENT AT ABOUT THE FOURTH MINUTE OF THE SECOND PULLING AND HE DID NOT GET IT BACK, AND HE TOLD ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE IN THAT YARD ELEVEN MINUTES AFTER THE SOUND STOPPED, BECAUSE HE HAD SAID ON THE TWENTY-NINTH OF THE SEVENTH MONTH THAT HE WOULD RATHER EVERYBODY KNEW A THING ABOUT HIS BODY ON A DAY HE NAMED THAN HAVE IT COME OUT OF A CONVERSATION ON SOME LATER AFTERNOON. THIS IS THE BODY COST PAID IN A SCENE IN THIS BLOCK AND IT IS THE FIRST SUCH COST THIS MANUSCRIPT HAS PAID TO A PERSON WHO IS NOT THE PROTAGONIST.** He said the name once, on the twenty-fifth, in a room, in the ordinary voice, and there is one more in that night behind the twenty-fourth of the sixth month and he will not say whether that one is a name or a hand. He carried nothing on the morning of the twenty-eighth and said so out loud in that yard, and nobody in that yard said anything about it. He filled two buckets and carried them up a bank to the building with two doors. **A MAN WHOSE TRADE IS OVER IS NOT A FAILURE AND IS NOT A TRAGIC FIGURE AND IS NOT REDEEMED, AND A CLERK MAY NOT SAY THAT ABOUT HIM AND MAY NOT SAY THE OTHER THING EITHER. A MAN WHO CARRIES A MESSAGE IS NOT A KEEPER OF IT AND DID NOT BECOME ONE BY SAYING IT. HE IS STILL IN THIS DISTRICT AND THAT IS NOT A POST AND NOT A ROLE AND NOT A FIGURE ABOUT HIM.**

## The woman of fifty-eight

**She is the one the man of about thirty-four chose on the twenty-fourth of the eighth month, and the choosing is in a scene and is a real act, and the two people he did not choose and the reasons for not choosing them are on the page. She is the only person in this district who has carried a second bucket to a house in the ground in the shadow of a building eleven miles off and has never once put a question to the woman in that house in nineteen years, and she said so out loud in the room four hundred yards from that gate on the twenty-ninth of the sixth month and a clerk entered the whole of it. That house is not one of the four hundred and eleven, is not on any sheet, and is the ninth of the eleven and one of the five different ninths in this district, and a sixth was available to a clerk this morning and was not taken.** She heard a name in a room on the twenty-fifth and did not say it back and wrote nothing down and asked him nothing about it. **SHE SAID IT OUT LOAD IN A YARD ON THE TWENTY-SIXTH, COMING DOWN THE BANK AT ABOUT FIVE WITH TWO BUCKSETS THE WAY SHE DOES, BEFORE SHE ASKED ANYBODY FOR ANYTHING AND BEFORE ANYBODY HAD ASKED HER FOR ANYTHING, BECAUSE A NAME IN ONE PERSON'S HEAD IS NOT SAFE. THE TWO WORDS ARE NOT ON THE PAGE.** She went up that bank at about half past three on the twenty-ninth with an empty bucket and came down it at about five with a full one and stopped at the foot of it for about nine minutes with nobody there, and a clerk entered that she was not asked about the line in the second of the two books and that she was not asked about anything else.

## The man of fifty-six

**He said out loud in a yard at about five on the afternoon of the twenty-first that he is not afraid of the night and is afraid of the morning, which he said once before in the fifteenth year and had not said since, and a clerk entered that it was not counted. He came out of a field at about a quarter past two in the morning of the night of the twenty-first and was in the last of it, and said it a third time at about three in front of about nineteen people, and **a clerk entered that a shape noticed a second time is not a finding and a third time is a habit.** He has now heard that bell three times, been in a field twice, been in a yard once, gone and looked at it three times and been told nothing three times. **HE IS NOT A CLERK AND HAS SAID SO ABOUT HIMSELF ABOUT A DOZEN TIMES IN FOUR MONTHS, AND A CLERK IS NOT GOING TO PUT HIS SENTENCE IN A COLUMN AND IS NOT GOING TO PUT HIS NAME OVER IT.**

## The man of about thirty-four who digs loam

**He said no in about four seconds, in a lane on the twenty-first, to being asked to go and stand outside that building in the dark, with a reason of his own: that he has already been out once, over ground he had never walked, and that he is not a person anybody in this district should want holding a lantern in the dark tonight. That is a refusal with a reason and is not one of the eight refusals with no reason a clerk of a house has given and is not a ninth of eleven coppers a week and is not an eighth about the ninth holding. HIS ARM HAS NOT GONE ABOVE THE LEVEL OF THAT SHOULDER SINCE THE FIRST OF THE SEVENTH MONTH AND IT IS THE SAME THIS EVENING AS IT WAS ON THE FIRST OF THIS MONTH, AND IT IS NOT SPENT AGAIN IN THIS BLOCK, AND THAT IS DISCLOSED.** He said out loud in a yard on the twenty-seventh, in two hundred and nineteen words, that the second of the two terms holds in this district and cannot hold anywhere else, and that both of those are the same fact, and that they are not all right, and that they have got one line of type and about nineteen readers and that is the whole of the protection.

## The man of about thirty-four who keeps a road

**He refused the night in a lane on the twenty-first in about four seconds with a reason of his own and is a refusal with a reason. He is at the foot of that low wall on four of the ten days of this block and nobody in that yard asked him one question about eleven miles on any of them, and he said nothing about the eight days he was on the road in the seventh month to anybody in this district and has not been asked. HIS ELEVEN MILES AND THE DITCH ARE NOT PAID AGAIN IN THIS BLOCK AND THAT IS DISCLOSED RATHER THAN SMOOTHED OVER. HE IS NOT A BEARER OF THAT ROAD AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE ONE, AND A BLOCK THAT ASKS HIM ABOUT ELEVEN MILES ON THE LAST DAY OF A VOLUME HAS SPENT SOMETHING A LATER BLOCK OWED HIMSELF.**

## The man of about thirty-seven who puts tables up for anybody who will use them

**He asked the woman of fifty-eight one question in a yard on the twenty-sixth and was refused in about four seconds with a reason of his own, which was that if the two words went on the end of the table he sets up against a wall eleven miles off, a stranger could walk eleven miles of road and read them, and that he said in a yard in the sixth month that a sheet of print out where a person walks past it is worth more than a sheet in a room, and he is not going to be the man who takes back a thing he said out loud in a yard. HE LOSES THE USE OF BOTH HANDS AND IS ASKED NOTHING ABOUT IT. THE TENT IS STILL ROLLED ON THE BACK OF THAT HAND-CART AND HAS NOT GONE UP ON ANY DAY SINCE THE THIRTIETH OF THE SIXTH MONTH, AND IT IS AT THE FOOT OF THE EAST WALL ON SEVEN OF THE TEN DAYS.**

## The man of about twenty-three who sells nothing and copies for nothing

**He stands about nine feet off the woman of about thirty-four who keeps a goat at the foot of that gatepost on the twenty-eighth while she reads the two sheets on it, and neither of them said a word. HE WAS OFFERED NOTHING THAT AFTERNOON AND COPIED NOTHING. HIS HAND HAS NOT CLOSED SINCE THE THIRTEENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND IS NOT SPENT AGAIN IN THIS BLOCK.**

**SHE IS NOT ASKED IN THIS BLOCK.** She is at the bank on the twenty-fourth with about nine sacks weighed one after another and the blanket of the scale folded over her arm, which she does not do, and he did not go down that lane to her. HER THREE WORDS ARE *RATE*, *EVEN* AND *STOCK*, AND NONE OF THE THREE IS SAID IN THIS BLOCK AND NONE OF THEM IS EXPLAINED BY HER, AND NO FOURTH WORD IS TO BE MANUFACTURED FOR HER.**

# Volume 07 Batch 0004 — the review-fix pass: what the repair did to the people, and what it did not do to anybody

> **A REVIEW OF CHAPTERS 331 TO 340 FOUND NINE THINGS AND ALL NINE ARE REPAIRED; THE RECORD IS `state/volume-07-batch-0004-summary.md` SECTION 10. NO CHARACTER STATE CHANGED EXCEPT IN THE TWO PLACES NAMED BELOW, AND NO AGE CHANGED ANYWHERE IN THE FORTY CHAPTERS OF VOLUME 07.**

## The one thing that is a state change, and it is the same in every chapter it appears in

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR.** **HIS STAY AND THE NIGHTS OF HIS RUN THAT HAVE BEGUN ARE NOW THE SAME FIGURE, AND HE HAS SLEPT ON ALL OF THEM AT CHAPTERS 331 AND 332 AND ON ONE FEWER FROM CHAPTER 333 ONWARD: THIRTY OF THIRTY, THIRTY-ONE OF THIRTY-ONE, THIRTY-ONE OF THIRTY-TWO, THIRTY-TWO OF THIRTY-THREE, THIRTY-THREE OF THIRTY-FOUR, THIRTY-FOUR OF THIRTY-FIVE, THIRTY-FIVE OF THIRTY-SIX, THIRTY-SIX OF THIRTY-SEVEN, THIRTY-SEVEN OF THIRTY-EIGHT, THIRTY-EIGHT OF THIRTY-NINE. THE ONE HE DID NOT SLEEP ON IS THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FIRST, WHEN HE STOOD IN THE SOUND OF THE BELL AND DID NOT LIE DOWN. THE FIRST TEXT PRINTED THIRTY OF THE THIRTY-ONE NIGHTS AT CHAPTER 331 AND THIRTY-NINE OF THE FORTY AT CHAPTER 340, AND A CARD THAT DECLARED THE RULE DECLARED IT WRONG.** He is still in this district thirty-nine days in on the last day of the volume, still with nothing in his hands, still not named, still not asked for a name, and **still a man whose trade is over and not a failure and not a tragic figure and not redeemed, which a clerk may not say about him and may not say the other thing either.** **THE EAR, THE ONE HE LOST ON THE LEFT SIDE AT ABOUT THE FOURTH MINUTE AND DID NOT GET BACK, IS NOW ON THE PAGE IN THE NIGHT IT HAPPENED AND NOT ONLY IN THE MORNING AFTER: at about the fourth minute he put his right hand flat on the left side of his head and did not take it off again that night, and about nine people in that yard were near enough to see him do it and not one of them said a word about it. HE STILL SAYS IT OUT LOUD ELEVEN MINUTES AFTER IT STOPPED, BECAUSE HE NAMED THE DAY, AND THE NAMED DAY WAS THE RIGHT ONE.** And at Chapter 336, in a scene, he is at the foot of that low wall from about ten until about half past two with his back to the sun, and at about eleven he turns his head to the left and holds it there and it does not get any better that way, and he turns it back to the yard.

## The three men of about thirty-four: the same three men, and the page now tells them apart

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A ROAD AND THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM. ALL THREE KEEP THAT AGE AND NO AGE WAS CHANGED IN ANY CHAPTER OF VOLUME 07 BY THIS PASS.** What changed is that the three of them can now be told apart on the page, and this is how: **THE MENDER IS THE ONE WITH A CLOTH IN HIS LEFT HAND AND A KNOT IN ABOUT NINE INCHES OF ROPE; THE MAN WHO KEEPS A ROAD IS THE ONE WHO WALKED THE EDGE OF THAT SOUND OUT ELEVEN MILES IN THE SEVENTH MONTH; THE MAN WHO DIGS LOAM IS THE ONE WHOSE RIGHT ARM HAS NOT GONE ABOVE THE LEVEL OF THAT SHOULDER SINCE THE FIRST OF THE SEVENTH MONTH.** Where two or more of them stand in one passage, the age is now dropped from all but the first of them in that chapter and the trade carries the rest.

At Chapter 342 that a description rehearsed on a bank is not the same thing in his hands, and that he had a use for an answer he knew he would not be given. At Chapter 345 that the namelessness had to go into a yard because in a room it would be a document and a document could be read back from the top by somebody, and that the whole sentence rested on nobody in that yard being willing to be the first to put a name in the air, and that he heard all four mouths open and close. HE IS NOT A KEEPER OF A READING, NOT A READER, NOT A POST, NOT A HOLDER, NOT NAMED, AND NO COLUMN WAS FILLED WITH HIM IN IT.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN WHO PUTS TABLES UP FOR ANYBODY WHO WILL USE THEM. ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THIS MONTH HE CAME OFF THE BRICK AT THE FOOT OF THE EAST WALL AT ABOUT HALF PAST TEN AND STOOD AT THE END OF THAT TRESTLE TABLE, WHICH HE HAD NOT DONE IN THREE WEEKS, AND HE WAS NOT ASKED TO, AND HE READ THE FOUR FIGURES OUT LOUD AND GOT THE LENGTH OF THIS MONTH WRONG BY ONE. THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX TOLD HIM OUT LOUD IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE AND HE SAID THANK YOU AND NOTHING ELSE AND STAYED AT THE END OF THAT TABLE FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES WITH HIS HANDS WHERE HE KEEPS THEM AND DID NOT GO AND GET HIS HAND-CART AND LEAVE. HIS HANDS WERE NOT TOUCHED AND NOTHING WAS ASKED ABOUT THEM. HE WAS GIVEN NOTHING, HIS TENT IS STILL ROLLED ON THE BACK OF THAT HAND-CART, IT HAS NOT GONE UP ON ANY DAY SINCE THE THIRTIETH OF THE SIXTH MONTH, AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD ASKED HIM ABOUT IT ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN WHO CUTS REEDS OUT OF THAT DITCH. HE IS IN THE BOTTOM OF THAT DITCH ON FIVE OF THE TEN DAYS AND ON THE TWENTY-EIGHTH HE WAS ASKED, NINE TIMES AND ONE AFTER ANOTHER AND IN THE ORDINARY WAY, WHETHER THAT BUILDING IS GOING TO BE STANDING IN THE WINTER, AND HE GAVE THE SAME ANSWER IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS EACH TIME, AND THE SAME ANSWER GIVEN NINE TIMES IN AN AFTERNOON IS A SHAPE NOTICED NINE TIMES.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR. THERE IS NOTHING IN HIS HANDS ON ALL TEN DAYS. ON THE TWENTY-SIXTH HE WAS ASLEEP ON THAT LOW WALL AT ABOUT HALF PAST NINE AT NIGHT WHEN THE MAN WHO KEEPS A ROAD GOT BACK TO IT AND DID NOT WAKE UP. NOBODY IN ANY YARD OF THIS BLOCK SAID ONE WORD TO HIM ABOUT WHAT HE IS GOING TO DO WITH THEM, AND HE IS NOT A KEEPER OF A LOT, NOT A BEARER OF THE READING, NOT THE SECOND READER, NOT A POST, NOT A STEWARD AND NOT A JOB NOBODY ASKED HIM FOR.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT WHO KEEPS A TALLY. HE IS IN NO SCENE OF THIS BLOCK. HIS FIGURE IS ENTERED ON ONE DAY, 367, AT A HUNDRED AND FIFTY-SEVEN DAYS, OUT OF A LADDER, AND HE IS NAMED THERE AND ASKED FOR NOTHING.**

**THE CHILD OF ABOUT EIGHT. HE IS NOT IN THIS BLOCK AND WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING BY ANYBODY IN IT.**
**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO KEEPS A SCALE. SHE IS NOT IN THIS BLOCK AND WAS NOT ASKED, AND HER FOUR EMPTY HOUSES WERE NOT OFFERED AND WERE NOT COUNTED.**

**AND THE ONE RELATIONSHIP THE BLOCK HAS. IT DID NOT MOVE AND IT DID NOT RESOLVE AND IT WAS NOT CHARACTERISED AS A CHAIR. ON THE TWENTY-FIRST OF THIS MONTH A WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT DESCRIBED THE ROOM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS UP THAT BANK FROM THE FOOT OF IT — THE SHUT DOOR, THE STEP OF TWO STONES, THE LIGHT OVER IT THAT DOES NOT LIGHT ANYTHING, AND THE FACT THAT A PERSON WHO HAS NOT BEEN LET IN DOES NOT KNOW THAT A PAGE IS IN THERE AT ALL — AND THE CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS DID NOT ANSWER HER, AND ON THE TWENTY-EIGHTH THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST WENT UP THAT LANE AS FAR AS THE FOOT OF THAT BANK AND STOOD THERE FOR ABOUT FOUR MINUTES AND DID NOT GO UP IT, AND HE DID NOT ASK ANYBODY WHERE THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THOSE TWO BOOKS IS, AND THE SECOND OF THE TWO BOOKS WAS NAMED ONCE, IN A LEDGER ENTRY THAT SAYS IT WAS NOT TOUCHED AND THAT A CLERK DID NOT PUT THAT DOWN EITHER. NEITHER OF THEM SAYS A DECLARATION AND THE BLOCK DOES NOT END ON A SETTLEMENT.**

**THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST, AND THE REGISTRAR OF THIS DISTRICT'S RECORDS OFFICE. HE IS IN ALL TEN CHAPTERS AND SPEAKS IN FOUR. HE WENT INTO A BUILDING WITH TWO DOORS FOR TWO AND A HALF HOURS, LEFT THE DOOR OPEN, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO WRITE A RULE, NOT GOING TO ASK THE MAN WITH THE CART, AND NOT GOING TO NAME ANYBODY. HE WENT DOWN ON HIS RIGHT KNEE IN THE MUD TO GET A RECORD OUT FROM UNDER THE LEG OF A TABLE, COULD NOT RISE IN ONE MOVEMENT, AND GOT UP IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH NOBODY OFFERING AN ARM. HIS FIGURE IS ENTERED ON FIVE OF THE TEN DAYS OUT OF A LADDER, HE WAS NOT ASKED FOR IT ON ANY OF THEM, AND HE IS A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT AND NOT THIRTY-FOUR AND HAS SAID OF HIMSELF THAT HE IS FIFTY-ONE.**
